7b:T1571,<p>All <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref> have an internal slot called <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var>. The value of this internal slot is either <emu-val>null</emu-val> or an object and is used for implementing inheritance. Assume a property named <var>propertyKey</var> is missing from an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> <var>obj</var> but exists on its <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> object. If <var>propertyKey</var> refers to a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref> on the <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> object, <var>obj</var> inherits it for get access, making it behave as if <var>propertyKey</var> was a property of <var>obj</var>. If <var>propertyKey</var> refers to a writable <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref> on the <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> object, set access of <var>propertyKey</var> on <var>obj</var> creates a new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref> named <var>propertyKey</var> on <var>obj</var>. If <var>propertyKey</var> refers to a non-writable <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref> on the <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> object, set access of <var>propertyKey</var> on <var>obj</var> fails. If <var>propertyKey</var> refers to an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">accessor property</a></emu-xref> on the <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> object, the accessor is inherited by <var>obj</var> for both get access and set access.</p>
    <p>Every <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> has a Boolean-valued <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> internal slot which is used to fulfill the extensibility-related internal method invariants specified in <emu-xref href="#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods">6.1.7.3</a></emu-xref>. Namely, once the value of an object's <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> internal slot has been set to <emu-val>false</emu-val>, it is no longer possible to add properties to the object, to modify the value of the object's <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> internal slot, or to subsequently change the value of <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> to <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</p>
    <p>In the following algorithm descriptions, assume <var>obj</var> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref>, <var>propertyKey</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> value, <var>value</var> is any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>, and <var>propertyDesc</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> record.</p>
    <p>Each <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> internal method delegates to a similarly-named abstract operation. If such an abstract operation depends on another internal method, then the internal method is invoked on <var>obj</var> rather than calling the similarly-named abstract operation directly. These semantics ensure that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic objects</a></emu-xref> have their overridden internal methods invoked when <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> internal methods are applied to them.</p>

92:Tbac,<p>The abstract operation OrdinarySetPrototypeOf takes arguments <var>obj</var> (an Object) and <var>proto</var> (an Object or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) and returns a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>current</var> be <var>obj</var>.<var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <emu-xref aoid="SameValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-samevalue">SameValue</a></emu-xref>(<var>proto</var>, <var>current</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>extensible</var> be <var>obj</var>.<var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>extensible</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>cursor</var> be <var>proto</var>.</li><li>Let <var>done</var> be <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li id="step-ordinarysetprototypeof-loop">Repeat, while <var>done</var> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>,<ol><li>If <var>cursor</var> is <emu-val>null</emu-val>, then<ol><li>Set <var>done</var> to <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li></ol></li><li>Else if <emu-xref aoid="SameValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-samevalue">SameValue</a></emu-xref>(<var>cursor</var>, <var>obj</var>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, then<ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>If <var>cursor</var>.<var class="field">[[GetPrototypeOf]]</var> is not the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> internal <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>Else, set <var>cursor</var> to <var>cursor</var>.<var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var>.</li></ol></li></ol></li><li>Set <var>obj</var>.<var         <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">

93:T588,<p>The <var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var> internal method of an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> <var>obj</var> takes argument <var>propertyKey</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref>) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>.       <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryGetOwnProperty"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinarygetownproperty">OrdinaryGetOwnProperty</a></emu-xref>(<var>obj</var>, <var>propertyKey</var>).</li></ol></emu-alg>

      94:Tb88,<p>The abstract operation OrdinaryGetOwnProperty takes arguments <var>obj</var> (an Object) and <var>propertyKey</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref>) and returns a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
        <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>obj</var> does not have an own property with key <var>propertyKey</var>, return <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>propertyDesc</var> be a newly created <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> with no fields.</li><li>Let <var>ownProperty</var> be <var>obj</var>'s own property whose key is <var>propertyKey</var>.</li><li>If <var>ownProperty</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref>, then<ol><li>Set <var>propertyDesc</var>.<var class="field">[[Value]]</var> to the value of <var>ownProperty</var>'s <var class="field">[[Value]]</var> attribute.</li><li>Set <var>propertyDesc</var>.<var class="field">[[Writable]]</var> to the value of <var>ownProperty</var>'s <var class="field">[[Writable]]</var> attribute.</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>ownProperty</var> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">accessor property</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Set <var>propertyDesc</var>.<var class="field">[[Get]]</var> to the value of <var>ownProperty</var>'s <var class="field">[[Get]]</var> attribute.</li><li>Set <var>propertyDesc</var>.<var class="field">[[Set]]</var> to the value of <var>ownProperty</var>'s <var class="field">[[Set]]</var> attribute.</li></ol></li><li>Set <var>propertyDesc</var>.<var class="field">[[Enumerable]]</var> to the value of <var>ownProperty</var>'s <var a4:Tcda,<p>The abstract operation OrdinaryObjectCreate takes argument <var>proto</var> (an Object or <emu-val>null</emu-val>) and optional argument <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> of names of internal slots) and returns an Object. It is used to specify the runtime creation of new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref>. <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var> contains the names of additional internal slots that must be defined as part of the object, beyond <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> and <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var>. If <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var> is not provided, a new empty <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> is used. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>internalSlotsList</var> be « <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> ».</li><li>If <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var> is present, set <var>internalSlotsList</var> to the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#list-concatenation">list-concatenation</a></emu-xref> of <var>internalSlotsList</var> and <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var>.</li><li>Let <var>obj</var> be <emu-xref aoid="MakeBasicObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-makebasicobject">MakeBasicObject</a></emu-xref>(<var>internalSlotsList</var>).</li><li>Set <var>obj</var>.<var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> to <var>proto</var>.</li><li class="exit">Return <var>obj</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Although OrdinaryObjectCreate does little more than call <emu-xref aoid="MakeBasicObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-makebasicobject">MakeBasicObject</a></emu-xref>, its use communicates the intention to create an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref>, and not an exotic one. Thus, within this specification, it is not called by any algorithm that subsequently modifies the internal methods of the object in ways that would make the result non-ordinary. Operations that create <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic objects</a></emu-xref> invoke <emu-xref aoid="MakeBasicObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-makebasicobject">MakeBasicObject</a></emu-xref> directly.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

a6:Tdd7,<p>The abstract operation GetPrototypeFromConstructor takes arguments <var>ctor</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>) and <var>intrinsicDefaultProto</var> (a String) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> an Object or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. It determines the <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> value that should be used to create an object corresponding to a specific <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref>.       <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>If <var>ctor</var> does not supply a <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> value, the default value that is used is obtained from the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> of the <var>ctor</var> function rather than from the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
    89:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-getprototypefromconstructor","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"10114-getprototypefromconstructor--ctor-intrinsicdefaultproto-","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.1.14"}]," GetPrototypeFromConstructor ( ",["$","var",null,{"children":"ctor"}],", ",["$","var",null,{"children":"intrinsicDefaultProto"}]," )"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"10114-getprototypefromconstructor--ctor-intrinsicdefaultproto-"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$a6"}}]]}]
a7:T6f1,<p>The abstract operation RequireInternalSlot takes arguments <var>obj</var> (an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref>) and <var>internalSlot</var> (an internal slot name) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> <emu-const>unused</emu-const> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">throw completion</a></emu-xref>. It throws an exception unless <var>obj</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">is an Object</a></emu-xref> and has the given internal slot. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <var>obj</var> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">is not an Object</a></emu-xref>, throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</li><li class="exit">If <var>obj</var> does not have an <var>internalSlot</var> internal slot, throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
    8a:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-requireinternalslot","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"10115-requireinternalslot--obj-internalslot-","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.1.15"}]," RequireInternalSlot ( ",["$","var",null,{"children":"obj"}],", ",["$","var",null,{"children":"internalSlot"}]," )"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"10115-requireinternalslot--obj-internalslot-"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$a7"}}]]}]
a8:T3a4c,<p>ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> encapsulate parameterized ECMAScript code closed over a lexical environment and support the dynamic evaluation of that code. An ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> and has the same internal slots and the same internal methods as other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref>. The code of an ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> may be either <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">strict mode code</a></emu-xref> (<emu-xref href="#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">11.2.2</a></emu-xref>) or <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#non-strict-code">non-strict code</a></emu-xref>. An ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> whose code is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">strict mode code</a></emu-xref> is called a <dfn id="strict-function" variants="strict functions">strict function</dfn>. One whose code is not <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-strict-mode-code">strict mode code</a></emu-xref> is called a <dfn id="non-strict-function" variants="non-strict functions">non-strict function</dfn>.</p>
    <p>In addition to <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> and <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var>, ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> also have the internal slots listed in <emu-xref href="#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects">Table 25</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <p>All ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> have the <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> internal method defined here. ECMAScript functions that are also <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructors</a></emu-xref> in addition have the <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method.</p>

    8b:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-ecmascript-function-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"102-ecmascript-function-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.2"}]," ECMAScript Function Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"102-ecmascript-function-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$a8"}}],"$La9","$Laa","$Lab","$Lac","$Lad","$Lae","$Laf","$Lb0","$Lb1","$Lb2","$Lb3"]}]
b4:T1333,<p>A built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref>; it must satisfy the requirements for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref> set out in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>In addition to the internal slots required of every <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> (see <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>), a built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> must also have the following internal slots:</p>
    <ul>
      <li><var class="field">[[Realm]]</var>, a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm-record">Realm Record</a></emu-xref> that represents the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#realm">realm</a></emu-xref> in which the function was created.</li>
      <li><var class="field">[[InitialName]]</var>, a String that is the initial name of the function. It is used by <emu-xref href="#sec-function.prototype.tostring"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-function.prototype.tostring">20.2.3.5</a></emu-xref>.</li>
      <li><var class="field">[[Async]]</var>, a Boolean that indicates whether the function has async function call and construct behaviour in <emu-xref aoid="BuiltinCallOrConstruct"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-builtincallorconstruct">BuiltinCallOrConstruct</a></emu-xref>.</li>
    </ul>
    <p>The initial value of a built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>'s <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> internal slot is <emu-intrinsic>%Function.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>, unless otherwise specified.</p>
    <p>A built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> must have a <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> internal method that conforms to the definition in <emu-xref href="#sec-built-in-function-objects-call-thisargument-argumentslist"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-built-in-function-objects-call-thisargument-argumentslist">10.3.1</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>A built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> has a <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method if and only if it is described as a “<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref>”, or some algorithm in this specification explicitly sets its <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method. Such a <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method must conform to the definition in <emu-xref href="#sec-built-in-function-objects-construct-argumentslist-newtarget"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-built-in-function-objects-construct-argumentslist-newtarget">10.3.2</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    <p>An implementation may provide additional built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> that are not defined in this specification.</p>

    8c:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-built-in-function-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"103-built-in-function-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.3"}]," Built-in Function Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"103-built-in-function-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$b4"}}],"$Lb5","$Lb6","$Lb7","$Lb8"]}]
b9:T15ae,<p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object">bound function exotic object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that wraps another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>. A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object">bound function exotic object</a></emu-xref> is callable (it has a <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> internal method and may have a <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal method). Calling a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object">bound function exotic object</a></emu-xref> generally results in a call of its wrapped function.</p>

      <p>An object is a <dfn id="bound-function-exotic-object" variants="bound function exotic objects">bound function exotic object</dfn> if its <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> and (if applicable) <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var> internal methods use the following implementations, and its other essential internal methods use the definitions found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed in <emu-xref aoid="BoundFunctionCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-boundfunctioncreate">BoundFunctionCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#bound-function-exotic-object">Bound function exotic objects</a></emu-xref> do not have the internal slots of ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function objects</a></emu-xref> listed in <emu-xref href="#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects">Table 25</a></emu-xref>. Instead they have the internal slots listed in <emu-xref href="#table-internal-slots-of-bound-function-exotic-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-internal-slots-of-bound-function-exotic-objects">Table 26</a></emu-xref>, in addition to <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> and <var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var>.</p>

c3:T1bb7,<p>A Proxy object is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> whose essential internal methods are partially implemented using ECMAScript code. Every Proxy object has an internal slot called <var class="field">[[ProxyHandler]]</var>. The value of <var class="field">[[ProxyHandler]]</var> is an object, called the proxy's <em>handler object</em>, or <emu-val>null</emu-val>. Methods (see <emu-xref href="#table-proxy-handler-methods"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-proxy-handler-methods">Table 29</a></emu-xref>) of a handler object may be used to augment the implementation for one or more of the Proxy object's internal methods. Every Proxy object also has an internal slot called <var class="field">[[ProxyTarget]]</var> whose value is either an object or <emu-val>null</emu-val>. This object is called the proxy's <em>target object</em>.</p>

    <p>An object is a <dfn id="proxy-exotic-object" variants="Proxy exotic objects">Proxy exotic object</dfn> if its essential internal methods (including <var class="field">[[Call]]</var> and <var class="field">[[Construct]]</var>, if applicable) use the definitions in this section. These internal methods are installed in <emu-xref aoid="ProxyCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-proxycreate">ProxyCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

    <p>When a handler method is called to provide the implementation of a Proxy object internal method, the handler method is passed the proxy's target object as a parameter. A proxy's handler object does not necessarily have a method corresponding to every essential internal method. Invoking an internal method on the proxy results in the invocation of the corresponding internal method on the proxy's target object if the handler object does not have a method corresponding to the internal trap.</p>
    <p>The <var class="field">[[ProxyHandler]]</var> and <var class="field">[[ProxyTarget]]</var> internal slots of a Proxy object are always initialized when the object is created and typically may not be modified. Some Proxy objects are created in a manner that permits them to be subsequently <em>revoked</em>. When a proxy is revoked, its <var class="field">[[ProxyHandler]]</var> and <var class="field">[[ProxyTarget]]</var> internal slots are set to <emu-val>null</emu-val> causing subsequent invocations of internal methods on that Proxy object to throw a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception.</p>
    <p>Because Proxy objects permit the implementation of internal methods to be provided by arbitrary ECMAScript code, it is possible to define a Proxy object whose handler methods violates the invariants defined in <emu-xref href="#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods">6.1.7.3</a></emu-xref>. Some of the internal method invariants defined in <emu-xref href="#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods">6.1.7.3</a></emu-xref> are essential integrity invariants. These invariants are explicitly enforced by the Proxy object internal methods specified in this section. An ECMAScript implementation must be robust in the presence of all possible invariant violations.</p>
    <p>In the following algorithm descriptions, assume <var>obj</var> is an ECMAScript Proxy object, <var>propertyKey</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> value, <var>value</var> is any <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-ecmascript-language-types">ECMAScript language value</a></emu-xref> and <var>propertyDesc</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> record.</p>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>When an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> is established for evaluating an ECMAScript function a new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-function-environment-records">Function Environment Record</a></emu-xref> is created and bindings for each formal parameter are instantiated in that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref>. Each declaration in the function body is also instantiated. If the function's formal parameters do not include any default value initializers then the body declarations are instantiated in the same <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> as the parameters. If default value parameter initializers exist, a second <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-environment-records">Environment Record</a></emu-xref> is created for the body declarations. Formal parameters and functions are initialized as part of FunctionDeclarationInstantiation. All other bindings are initialized during evaluation of the function body.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

This operation creates a built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>If <var>realm</var> is not present, set <var>realm</var> to <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#current-realm"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#current-realm">the current Realm Record</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>If <var>proto</var> is not present, set <var>proto</var> to <var>realm</var>.<var class="field">[[Intrinsics]]</var>.[[<emu-intrinsic>%Function.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>]].</li><li>If <var>async</var> is not present, set <var>async</var> to <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li>Let <var>internalSlotsList</var> be a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> containing the names of all the internal slots that <emu-xref href="#sec-built-in-function-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-built-in-function-objects">10.3</a></emu-xref> requires for the built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> that is about to be created.</li><li>Append the elements of <var>additionalInternalSlotsList</var> to <var>internalSlotsList</var>.</li><li>Let <var>func</var> be a new built-in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> that, when called, performs the action described by <var>behaviour</var> using the provided arguments as the values of the corresponding parameters specified by <var>behaviour</var>. The new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> has internal slots whose names are the elements of <var>internalSlotsList</var>, and an <var class="field">[[InitialName]]</var> internal slot.</li><li>Set <var>func</var>.<var class="field">[[Async]]</var> to <var>async</var>.</li><li>Set <var>func</var>.<var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> to <var>proto</var>.</li><li>Set <var>func</var>.<var class="field">[[Extensible]]</var> to <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li>Set <var>func</var>.<var class="field">[[Realm]]</var> to <var>realm</var>.</li><li>Set <var>func</var>.<var class="field">[[InitialName]]</var> to <emu-val>null</emu-val>.</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="SetFunctionLength"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-setfunctionlength">SetFunctionLength</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>, <var>length</var>).</li><li>If <var>prefix</var> is not present, then<ol><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="SetFunctionName"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-setfunctionname">SetFunctionName</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>, <var>name</var>).</li></ol></li><li>Else,<ol><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="SetFunctionName"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-setfunctionname">SetFunctionName</a></emu-xref>(<var>func</var>, <var>name</var>, <var>prefix</var>).</li></ol></li><li class="exit">Return <var>func</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <p>Each built-in function defined in this specification is created by calling the CreateBuiltinFunction abstract operation.</p>

eb:T1025,<p>An Array is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that gives special treatment to <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property keys</a></emu-xref> (see <emu-xref href="#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">6.1.7</a></emu-xref>). A property whose <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-name">property name</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref> is also called an <em>element</em>. Every Array has a non-configurable <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property whose value is always a non-negative <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integral-number"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integral-number">integral Number</a></emu-xref> whose <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#mathematical-value"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#mathematical-value">mathematical value</a></emu-xref> is strictly less than 2<sup>32</sup>. The value of the <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property is numerically greater than the name of every own property whose name is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref>; whenever an own property of an Array is created or changed, other properties are adjusted as necessary to maintain this invariant. Specifically, whenever an own property is added whose name is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref>, the value of the <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property is changed, if necessary, to be one more than the numeric value of that <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref>; and whenever the value of the <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property is changed, every own property whose name is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref> whose value is not smaller than the new length is deleted. This constraint applies only to own properties of an Array and is unaffected by <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> or <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref> properties that may be inherited from its prototypes.</p>

      <p>An object is an <dfn id="array-exotic-object" variants="Array exotic objects">Array exotic object</dfn> (or simply, an Array) if its <var class="field">[[DefineOwnProperty]]</var> internal method uses the following implementation, and its other essential internal methods use the definitions found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed in <emu-xref aoid="ArrayCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-arraycreate">ArrayCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      bd:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-array-exotic-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1042-array-exotic-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.4.2"}]," Array Exotic Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"1042-array-exotic-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$eb"}}],"$Lec","$Led","$Lee","$Lef"]}]
f0:Tb68,<p>A String object is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that encapsulates a String value and exposes virtual <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index">integer-indexed</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data properties</a></emu-xref> corresponding to the individual code unit elements of the String value. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#string-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#string-exotic-object">String exotic objects</a></emu-xref> always have a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data property</a></emu-xref> named <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> whose value is the length of the encapsulated String value. Both the code unit <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data properties</a></emu-xref> and the <emu-val>"length"</emu-val> property are non-writable and non-configurable.</p>

      <p>An object is a <dfn id="string-exotic-object" variants="String exotic objects">String exotic object</dfn> (or simply, a String object) if its <var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[DefineOwnProperty]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[OwnPropertyKeys]]</var> internal methods use the following implementations, and its other essential internal methods use the definitions found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed in <emu-xref aoid="StringCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-stringcreate">StringCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#string-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#string-exotic-object">String exotic objects</a></emu-xref> have the same internal slots as <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref>. They also have a <var class="field">[[StringData]]</var> internal slot.</p>

      be:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-string-exotic-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1043-string-exotic-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.4.3"}]," String Exotic Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"1043-string-exotic-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$f0"}}],"$Lf1","$Lf2","$Lf3","$Lf4","$Lf5"]}]
f6:T222a,<p>Most ECMAScript functions make an arguments object available to their code. Depending upon the characteristics of the function definition, its arguments object is either an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref> or an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic object</a></emu-xref>. An <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> whose <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#array-index">array index</a></emu-xref> properties map to the formal parameters bindings of an invocation of its associated ECMAScript function.</p>

      <p>An object is an <dfn id="arguments-exotic-object" variants="arguments exotic objects">arguments exotic object</dfn> if its internal methods use the following implementations, with the ones not specified here using those found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed in <emu-xref aoid="CreateMappedArgumentsObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-createmappedargumentsobject">CreateMappedArgumentsObject</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>While <emu-xref aoid="CreateUnmappedArgumentsObject"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-createunmappedargumentsobject">CreateUnmappedArgumentsObject</a></emu-xref> is grouped into this clause, it creates an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref>, not an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic object</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">Arguments exotic objects</a></emu-xref> have the same internal slots as <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref>. They also have a <var class="field">[[ParameterMap]]</var> internal slot. Ordinary arguments objects also have a <var class="field">[[ParameterMap]]</var> internal slot whose value is always <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>. For ordinary argument objects the <var class="field">[[ParameterMap]]</var> internal slot is only used by <code>Object.prototype.toString</code> (<emu-xref href="#sec-object.prototype.tostring"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/fundamental-objects#sec-object.prototype.tostring">20.1.3.6</a></emu-xref>) to identify them as such.</p>

      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index">integer-indexed</a></emu-xref> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">data properties</a></emu-xref> of an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic object</a></emu-xref> whose numeric name values are less than the number of formal parameters of the corresponding <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> initially share their values with the corresponding argument bindings in the function's <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref>. This means that changing the property changes the corresponding value of the argument binding and vice-versa. This correspondence is broken if such a property is deleted and then redefined or if the property is changed into an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">accessor property</a></emu-xref>. If the arguments object is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary object</a></emu-xref>, the values of its properties are simply a copy of the arguments passed to the function and there is no dynamic linkage between the property values and the formal parameter values.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The ParameterMap object and its property values are used as a device for specifying the arguments object correspondence to argument bindings. The ParameterMap object and the objects that are the values of its properties are not directly observable from ECMAScript code. An ECMAScript implementation does not need to actually create or use such objects to implement the specified semantics.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="4"><span class="note">Note 4</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Ordinary arguments objects define a non-configurable <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">accessor property</a></emu-xref> named <emu-val>"callee"</emu-val> which throws a <emu-val>TypeError</emu-val> exception on access. The <emu-val>"callee"</emu-val> property has a more specific meaning for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic objects</a></emu-xref>, which are created only for some class of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#non-strict-function">non-strict functions</a></emu-xref>. The definition of this property in the ordinary variant exists to ensure that it is not defined in any other manner by conforming ECMAScript implementations.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="5"><span class="note">Note 5</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>ECMAScript implementations of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#arguments-exotic-object">arguments exotic objects</a></emu-xref> have historically contained an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-object-type">accessor property</a></emu-xref> named <emu-val>"caller"</emu-val>. Prior to ECMAScript 2017, this specification included the definition of a throwing <emu-val>"caller"</emu-val> property on ordinary arguments objects. Since implementations do not contain this extension any longer, ECMAScript 2017 dropped the requirement for a throwing <emu-val>"caller"</emu-val> accessor.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

fe:Tf4f,<p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray">TypedArray</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that performs special handling of <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property keys</a></emu-xref> that are <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring">canonical numeric strings</a></emu-xref>, using the subset that are in-bounds <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#integer-index">integer indices</a></emu-xref> to index elements of uniform type and enforcing the invariant that the remainder are absent without incurring prototype chain traversal.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Because <emu-xref aoid="ToString"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-tostring">ToString</a></emu-xref>(<var>n</var>) for any Number <var>n</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/abstract-operations#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring">canonical numeric string</a></emu-xref>, an implementation may treat Numbers as <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property keys</a></emu-xref> for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray">TypedArrays</a></emu-xref> without actually performing the string conversion.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray">TypedArrays</a></emu-xref> have the same internal slots as <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#ordinary-object">ordinary objects</a></emu-xref> and additionally <var class="field">[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]</var>, <var class="field">[[TypedArrayName]]</var>, <var class="field">[[ContentType]]</var>, <var class="field">[[ByteLength]]</var>, <var class="field">[[ByteOffset]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[ArrayLength]]</var> internal slots.</p>
      <p>An object is a <dfn id="typedarray" oldids="integer-indexed-exotic-object" variants="TypedArrays">TypedArray</dfn> if its <var class="field">[[PreventExtensions]]</var>, <var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[HasProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[DefineOwnProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Get]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Set]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Delete]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[OwnPropertyKeys]]</var>, internal methods use the definitions in this section, and its other essential internal methods use the definitions found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed by <emu-xref aoid="TypedArrayCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-typedarraycreate">TypedArrayCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      c0:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-typedarray-exotic-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1045-typedarray-exotic-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.4.5"}]," TypedArray Exotic Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"1045-typedarray-exotic-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$fe"}}],"$Lff","$L100","$L101","$L102","$L103","$L104","$L105","$L106","$L107","$L108","$L109","$L10a","$L10b","$L10c","$L10d","$L10e","$L10f","$L110","$L111"]}]
112:T19ad,<p>A <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object">module namespace exotic object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that exposes the bindings exported from an ECMAScript <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt> (See <emu-xref href="#sec-exports"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#sec-exports">16.2.3</a></emu-xref>). There is a one-to-one correspondence between the String-keyed own properties of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object">module namespace exotic object</a></emu-xref> and the binding names exported by the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>. The exported bindings include any bindings that are indirectly exported using <code>export *</code> export items. Each String-valued own <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> is the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of the corresponding exported binding name. These are the only String-keyed properties of a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object">module namespace exotic object</a></emu-xref>. Each such property has the attributes { <var class="field">[[Writable]]</var>: <emu-val>true</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Enumerable]]</var>: <emu-val>true</emu-val>, <var class="field">[[Configurable]]</var>: <emu-val>false</emu-val> }. <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#module-namespace-exotic-object">Module namespace exotic objects</a></emu-xref> are not extensible.</p>
      <p>An object is a <dfn id="module-namespace-exotic-object" variants="module namespace exotic objects">module namespace exotic object</dfn> if its <var class="field">[[GetPrototypeOf]]</var>, <var class="field">[[SetPrototypeOf]]</var>, <var class="field">[[IsExtensible]]</var>, <var class="field">[[PreventExtensions]]</var>, <var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[DefineOwnProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[HasProperty]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Get]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Set]]</var>, <var class="field">[[Delete]]</var>, and <var class="field">[[OwnPropertyKeys]]</var> internal methods use the definitions in this section, and its other essential internal methods use the definitions found in <emu-xref href="#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots">10.1</a></emu-xref>. These methods are installed by <emu-xref aoid="ModuleNamespaceCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-modulenamespacecreate">ModuleNamespaceCreate</a></emu-xref>.</p>

      c1:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-module-namespace-exotic-objects","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1046-module-namespace-exotic-objects","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"10.4.6"}]," Module Namespace Exotic Objects"],["$","$L7a",null,{"id":"1046-module-namespace-exotic-objects"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$112"}}],"$L113","$L114","$L115","$L116","$L117","$L118","$L119","$L11a","$L11b","$L11c","$L11d","$L11e"]}]
11f:T94f,<p>An <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object">immutable prototype exotic object</a></emu-xref> is an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic object</a></emu-xref> that has a <var class="field">[[Prototype]]</var> internal slot that will not change once it is initialized.</p>

      <p>An object is an <dfn id="immutable-prototype-exotic-object" variants="immutable prototype exotic objects">immutable prototype exotic object</dfn> if its <var class="field">[[SetPrototypeOf]]</var> internal method uses the following implementation. (Its other essential internal methods may use any implementation, depending on the specific <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object">immutable prototype exotic object</a></emu-xref> in question.)</p>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>Unlike other <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#exotic-object">exotic objects</a></emu-xref>, there is not a dedicated creation abstract operation provided for <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#immutable-prototype-exotic-object">immutable prototype exotic objects</a></emu-xref>. This is because they are only used by <emu-intrinsic>%Object.prototype%</emu-intrinsic> and by <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref>, and in <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/overview#host-environment">host environments</a></emu-xref>, the relevant objects are potentially exotic in other ways and thus need their own dedicated creation operation.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var> for Proxy objects enforces the following invariants:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>
            The result of <var class="field">[[GetOwnProperty]]</var> must be either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type">Property Descriptor</a></emu-xref> or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>.
          </li>
          <li>
            A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as a non-configurable own property of the target object.
          </li>
          <li>
            A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as an own property of a non-extensible target object.
          </li>
          <li>
            A property cannot be reported as existent, if it does not exist as an own property of the target object and the target object is not extensible.
          </li>
          <li>
            A property cannot be reported as non-configurable, unless it exists as a non-configurable own property of the target object.
          </li>
          <li>
            A property cannot be reported as both non-configurable and non-writable, unless it exists as a non-configurable, non-writable own property of the target object.
          </li>
        </ul>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><var class="field">[[OwnPropertyKeys]]</var> for Proxy objects enforces the following invariants:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>
            The result of <var class="field">[[OwnPropertyKeys]]</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref>.
          </li>
          <li>
            The returned <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> contains no duplicate entries.
          </li>
          <li>
            Each element of the returned <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref>.
          </li>
          <li>
            The result <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> must contain the keys of all non-configurable own properties of the target object.
          </li>
          <li>
            If the target object is not extensible, then the result <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a></emu-xref> must contain all the keys of the own properties of the target object and no other values.
          </li>
        </ul>

14f:Td9e,<p>An <dfn variants="TypedArray With Buffer Witness Records">TypedArray With Buffer Witness Record</dfn> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a></emu-xref> value used to encapsulate a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#typedarray">TypedArray</a></emu-xref> along with a cached byte length of the viewed buffer. It is used to help ensure there is a single <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/memory-model#sec-memory-model-fundamentals">ReadSharedMemory</a></emu-xref> event of the byte length data block when the viewed buffer is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-fixed-length-and-growable-sharedarraybuffer-objects"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/structured-data#sec-fixed-length-and-growable-sharedarraybuffer-objects">growable SharedArrayBuffer</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        <p>TypedArray With Buffer Witness Records have the fields listed in <emu-xref href="#table-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-record-fields"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#table-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-record-fields">Table 27</a></emu-xref>.</p>
